**Reference layer.** The integrated governing spine above controls where older formulations differ in emphasis. This section is preserved because its detailed reasoning, counter-case, falsifiers and expert questions remain useful; it should not be read as a separate competing theory.
:::

## 129. Strongest case object after six-pass resynthesis {#129-strongest-case-object-after-six-pass-resynthesis .source-h1 .anchored-heading}

## 129.1 The object {#129-1-the-object .anchored-heading}

The strongest case object is no longer "fragmentation" and should not be reduced to "missed dystonia," "wrong neuropathy," or "missed PRKN." It is a longitudinal information-state problem:

> **A broad relational presentation was contracted into a narrower specialist task; the omitted material later re-entered as facts, but different parts of the phenotype were resolved at unequal evidential depth. That mixed state became actionable before all relationships were resolved. Service destinations and classifications then travelled more effectively than the uncertainty, provenance and re-entry conditions that originally qualified them. Later boundary-crossing evidence was repeatedly handled through local assimilation/reclassification rather than a clearly identifiable reconstruction of the parent model. By 2025 the accumulated documentary pathway itself was being used retrospectively to explain and defend management, while the disputed category genealogy remained less fully reconstructed.**

## 129.2 The 2017 fork {#129-2-the-2017-fork .anchored-heading}

The strongest 2017 internal contrast is now:

**High-resolution neurological branch:**

-   marked symmetrical tone/rigidity;
-   gross bradykinesia;
-   striking gait;
-   pseudo-foot-drop appearance described as somewhat dystonic and explicitly not true foot drop;
-   imaging/DaT investigation;
-   broad young-onset differential;
-   autosomal-recessive/monogenic reasoning;
-   specific PRKN/PARK2 suspicion;
-   outside specialist review.

**Lower-resolution residual branch:**

-   genuine corns/callouses/pes planus/hammertoes;
-   foot pain;
-   burning introduced in the specialist synthesis, with later patient-origin support that burning was real;
-   patient-reported distinction between foot pain and leg/bodily tension;
-   clinician composite "leg symptoms including corns and callouses and burning";
-   possible separate process / possible secondary peripheral neuropathy / "Not clear";
-   Podiatry destination.

The concern is not that these could not coexist. They plainly could. The documentary question is whether the surviving record shows the discriminating bridge by which the same lower-limb territory was partitioned among dystonic/Parkinsonian, structural/mechanical and peripheral-neuropathic explanations.

## 129.3 Provenance and grammatical status {#129-3-provenance-and-grammatical-status .anchored-heading}

The strongest provenance finding is that the patient\'s documented distinction is narrower than the later specialist category. The patient is recorded as distinguishing **foot pain/corns/callouses** from **leg/bodily tension**. The specialist synthesis then creates a broader object --- **"leg symptoms including corns and callouses and burning"** --- and attaches a possible separate/peripheral-neuropathic hypothesis.

This supports the following documentary chain, subject to the stated qualifications:

> **patient phenomena → clinician recomposition → tentative diagnostic hypothesis → named working question → later symptom classification → retrospective affirmative grammar**

The later 2023 severe bilateral burning episode is genuinely new evidence and prevents the neuropathic pathway being characterised as repetition alone. The narrower point is that fresh evidence and inherited category can coexist, and later repetition is not automatically independent revalidation of the original classification.

## 129.4 "As per above" as the operational hinge {#129-4-as-per-above-as-the-operational-hinge .anchored-heading}

"As per above" now has a precise role. It does not erase uncertainty and should not be treated as a declaration that every proposition was solved. Its function is:

> **to convert an information state containing both high-confidence and unresolved propositions into a workable management state.**

That allowed medication, referral, rehabilitation, Podiatry, investigations, follow-up and deferred genetics to proceed. The resulting longitudinal vulnerability was not tolerance of uncertainty itself; it was that once uncertainty no longer blocked action, unresolved relationships required a separate ownership/reopening mechanism if they were to remain decision-live.

## 129.5 Distributed residual ownership {#129-5-distributed-residual-ownership .anchored-heading}

By discharge, components had owners:

-   Neurology: Parkinsonism, treatment, imaging/genetics, follow-up;
-   Podiatry: structural foot care;
-   Physio/OT: rehabilitation and function;
-   Occupational Health: work capability;
-   GP: coordination, referrals, prescriptions and longitudinal observation.

What is less explicit is ownership of the relationships among those components. The strongest formulation remains:

> **The components were distributed, while responsibility for reconstructing their relationship is not comparably explicit.**

The GP trial strengthens this further:

> **The GP kept seeing the arrows; specialist care owned the authority to decide what they meant.**

## 129.6 Local updating versus parent-model reconstruction {#129-6-local-updating-versus-parent-model-reconstruction .anchored-heading}

Later evidence shows the inherited architecture was permeable:

-   treatment withdrawal linked feet, gait and tension temporally;
-   later distal power testing supported a Parkinsonian "foot throw";
-   OFF dystonia was explicitly considered;
-   abrupt overnight pain onset was recognised as atypical for peripheral neuropathy;
-   Fabry was tested as an overlap possibility;
-   pain varied with dopaminergic state;
-   pregabalin could reduce pain while toe curling persisted.

These are genuine reassessments. The narrow continuing finding is that they are easier to identify as **proposition-specific local updates** than as a single pre-2025 reconstruction of the founding feet--gait--posture--pain relationship.

## 129.7 March 2025 as retrospective test {#129-7-march-2025-as-retrospective-test .anchored-heading}

The March 2025 response is now best understood as a test of whether documentary continuity equals evidential genealogy. It contains substantial retrospective clinical reasoning, particularly around treatment choices, dyskinesia risk, Sinemet, pregabalin, Fabry and pain modulation. But it does not equivalently reconstruct how the contested categories obtained their present status.

For pain:

-   2017: possible separate process / perhaps secondary peripheral neuropathy / "Not clear";
-   later: "the neuropathy question" / "? neuropathic pain";
-   2025: "peripheral neuropathic pain symptomatologies that Liam has" and "neuropathic pain (of whatever cause)."

The strongest distinction is:

> **the 2025 response reopens the cause of the neuropathic category more clearly than it reopens whether the historical phenotype should itself be classified as neuropathic.**

For Parkinsonism:

-   syndrome-level continuity is strong;
-   etiology remained specifically open around PRKN/PARK2;
-   testing was deliberately deferred because it did not alter current management;
-   no clear pre-2025 documentary bridge has been identified showing PRKN rejection before affirmative iPD language became routine.

Thus:

> **The record demonstrates continuity of the Parkinsonian syndrome far more clearly than it demonstrates closure of its etiology.**

## 129.8 "As the letters show" and documentary accretion {#129-8-as-the-letters-show-and-documentary-accretion .anchored-heading}

The sequence now supported is:

> **mixed 2017 synthesis → "As per above" → actionable plan → inherited summaries ("as per older letters") → local updates → accumulated longitudinal documentation → retrospective invocation ("As the letters show").**

This is not inherently improper. Longitudinal medicine requires documentary inheritance. The information-governance question is narrower:

> **Can repeated documentary continuity increase the practical stability of a category even where the original evidential transition and its unresolved qualifiers are not repeatedly reconstructed?**

The working answer is yes as a documentary mechanism, with proposition-specific exceptions where later evidence genuinely revalidates a claim.

## 129.9 Strongest governance formulation {#129-9-strongest-governance-formulation .anchored-heading}

The strongest governance formulation is not "the diagnosis was wrong" and not "nobody cared for the feet." It is:

> **The system successfully resolved one high-priority diagnostic object but did not make equally clear who retained responsibility for the unresolved relationships left outside that object.**

A related professional information question is:

> **When a tentative specialist classification reorganises downstream care, how much of its provenance, evidential basis, uncertainty, competing evidence and reopening conditions must travel with the label so that repetition does not silently upgrade hypothesis into history?**

Whether any standard was breached remains an expert/legal question.

## 129.10 Strongest defence {#129-10-strongest-defence .anchored-heading}

The strongest defence remains substantial:

-   the patient genuinely had heterogeneous lower-limb phenomena;
-   structural foot disease was real;
-   symptom dissociations existed;
-   the Parkinsonian syndrome was strongly supported;
-   the consultant performed extensive investigation and later genuine reconsideration;
-   2023 is serious counterevidence to any claim of a frozen model;
-   later burning symptoms provided fresh grounds for neuropathic consideration;
-   medicine legitimately proceeds under uncertainty and does not require every causal relationship to be solved before treatment.

The competing explanation is therefore:

> **clinically appropriate decomposition of genuinely different phenomena, followed by reasonable longitudinal management within a well-supported Parkinsonian framework.**

The record alone cannot decide the medical validity of that defence.

## 129.11 Strongest falsifiers {#129-11-strongest-falsifiers .anchored-heading}

The case object would be materially weakened by any of the following contemporaneous evidence:

1.  **2017 lower-limb discriminating bridge:** bedside/ward notes showing detailed distal sensory mapping, proposition-specific motor testing, dynamic toe/posture assessment, structural/loading reasoning and an explicit explanation distinguishing dystonic/Parkinsonian, structural and neuropathic components.
2.  **2017 ownership/reopening plan:** complex-case/discharge documentation explicitly stating that the feet--gait--tone relationship remained unresolved, assigning an owner, requiring Podiatry feedback and defining a review trigger.
3.  **Independent early reconstruction:** a Beaumont/Movement Disorders assessment that received the original GP relational presentation, rebuilt it from first principles and independently arrived at substantially the same partition.
4.  **Pre-2025 global reconstruction:** a later specialist note explicitly recombining structural feet, pain/burning, dynamic toe posture, dystonic gait, OFF state, medication responses and neuropathic possibilities into a newly reasoned parent model.
5.  **PRKN closure bridge:** a pre-2025 source explicitly preserving PRKN as unresolved operational shorthand or showing why it was downgraded/excluded before affirmative idiopathic terminology became established.

## 129.12 Shortest protected formulations {#129-12-shortest-protected-formulations .anchored-heading}

The following should now be protected from drift unless primary evidence forces revision:

> **The critical transformation occurred not when the facts disappeared, but between their recovery and their synthesis.**

> **The record is less a story of facts being forgotten than of relationships being reformatted once, then inherited.**

> **The facts came back. The relationships did not come back with equal force.**

> **The sophisticated reasoning is not merely uneven in quantity; it differs in evidential resolution.**

> **"As per above" marks operational closure before epistemic closure was complete.**

> **The components were distributed, while responsibility for reconstructing their relationship is not comparably explicit.**

> **The GP kept seeing the arrows; specialist care owned the authority to decide what they meant.**

> **When the model was fresh, uncertainty generated questions; once the model was inherited, uncertainty was more often managed within it.**

> **By 2025 the response reconstructs management more completely than it reconstructs the evidential genealogy of the disputed classifications.**

> **The deepest issue is the conversion of an incompletely discriminated specialist classification into a durable organising structure for later facts.**

> **The record is unusually well equipped to defend the decisions that were made, but materially less well equipped to show that the relationships excluded from those decisions were equivalently tested.**

> **The retrospective defence may convert documentary continuity into evidential continuity.**

> **Do not infer recognised error, institutional protection, concealment or falsification without internal contemporaneous evidence demonstrating that transition.**

## 129.13 Endpoint discipline {#129-13-endpoint-discipline .anchored-heading}

The 2026 PRKN result remains an endpoint only. It establishes that Parkinsonism and dystonia ultimately received a common molecular explanation. It does not prove that every historical foot symptom was dystonic, that peripheral/structural explanations were necessarily wrong, or that earlier clinicians should have reached the molecular diagnosis. Its documentary significance is that it resolves a question the historical record itself had already generated and deliberately left open.

## 129.14 Therapeutic-phenotype consequence branch {#129-14-therapeutic-phenotype-consequence-branch .anchored-heading}

This branch is subordinate to the information-pathway case object. It does not convert the documentary case into a retrospective claim that the historical pain was PRKN-mediated, dystonic, or necessarily caused by under-treatment.

The expert-level question is narrower:

> **Did the inherited separation of the painful-foot territory from the dystonic/Parkinsonian territory later constrain whether a PRKN-relevant therapeutic phenotype was assembled and tested as a whole?**

The record contains a potentially important sequence:

-   PRKN/PARK2 was a specific contemporaneous hypothesis rather than a later invention;
-   the 2017 lower limb was directly described as dystonic-looking rather than true foot drop;
-   external specialist advice reportedly linked PARK2 to low-dose dopaminergic responsiveness and dyskinesia risk;
-   later levodopa exposure was constrained by dyskinesia/chorea;
-   later records contain possible OFF dystonia, insufficient-coverage reasoning, medication-timed lower-limb symptoms and pain modulation;
-   sustained/overnight dopaminergic coverage later changed the exposure pattern and was followed by reported marked morning improvement.

None of these propositions independently proves a common mechanism. Together they justify a movement-disorders expert question about whether the longitudinal record contains both a **dopaminergic ceiling** (dyskinesia at higher exposure) and possible **dopaminergic trough phenomena** (OFF dystonia, morning symptoms, pain or gait deterioration at lower/waning exposure), without a clearly visible unified therapeutic-window model of the feet.

The protected formulation is:

> **The question is not whether levodopa response proves the cause of pain. It is whether repeated medication-state effects, dystonic lower-limb findings and treatment-limiting dyskinesia should have triggered structured testing of a dose-state lower-limb hypothesis.**

A separate information-quality question concerns the 2021 DBS explanation. If the source wording is accurately preserved as DBS being "only ever for the tremor element" and not helping anything else, the expert question is not whether DBS should have been performed. It is:

> **Was the information supplied about the therapeutic scope of DBS sufficiently accurate for a young, levodopa-responsive patient already developing dyskinesia and motor-state complexity?**

This is deliberately kept separate from the foot-pain causation case. It is an information-quality / informed-discussion question requiring contemporaneous standards and expert review.

The strongest falsifiers for this branch would be contemporaneous notes showing that the treating team explicitly modelled and tested the lower-limb symptoms against ON/OFF state, formulation, timing and dyskinesia constraints, or that the apparent DBS wording is incomplete/misleading when read in full context.

## 129.15 Prospective defensibility, asymmetric epistemic documentation, and constructive retrospective defence {#129-15-prospective-defensibility-asymmetric-epistemic-documentation-and-constructive-retrospective-defence .anchored-heading}

A further documentary branch now requires explicit protection against both under-reading and overstatement.

The record contains unusually detailed contemporaneous explanations for decisions that later became central to the 2025 complaint response: why levodopa was delayed, why genetic confirmation might not alter immediate management, why the patient was said to understand the strategy, why external advice was sought, and even the contemporaneous possibility that the team might later regret not starting levodopa earlier.

The existence of such forward-looking reasoning does **not** establish that the record was written dishonestly or in anticipation of litigation. The strongest benign explanation remains **ordinary conscientious documentation of a difficult young-onset case**. A second plausible layer is **prospective defensibility**: deliberately recording why a difficult decision was made so that it could later be understood and defended. That can be entirely proper.

The serious documentary question begins one step later:

> **Did the record become progressively better at preserving the rationale for the decisions actually made than at preserving and re-testing the relationships excluded by those decisions?**

This is best described as **asymmetric epistemic documentation** or, where it affects later framing, **defensive problem-framing**. These terms do not imply dishonesty, concealment or motive.

The strongest supported formulation is:

> **The record is unusually well equipped to defend the decisions that were made, but materially less well equipped to show that the relationships excluded from those decisions were equivalently tested.**

A related asymmetry concerns dopaminergic management. The rationale for caution at the upper end of exposure is extensively preserved: long-term strategy, concern about early dyskinesia, external advice and later treatment-limiting chorea/dyskinesia. The corresponding lower-end question---whether restricted or waning exposure permitted OFF dystonia, gait failure or painful lower-limb phenomena---is less visibly developed as a unified model. This does not establish under-treatment. It identifies an asymmetry in what the record explains at high resolution.

### 129.15.1 March 2025 as an explicitly explanatory/defensive information product {#129-15-1-march-2025-as-an-explicitly-explanatory-defensive-information-product .anchored-heading}

The complaint response should be analysed as what it openly is: a retrospective explanatory document prepared for review, not an ordinary contemporaneous clinic note. There is nothing inherently improper about that function.

Its historical method nevertheless requires proposition-level discrimination. The following are not equivalent:

> **documentation existed → a subject was mentioned → a possibility was recognised → a proposition was investigated → a relationship was adjudicated → the resulting management was appropriate.**

The central risk is therefore not necessarily factual falsity. It is that a retrospective narrative may assemble true pieces into an argument whose logical force exceeds what the underlying historical evidence establishes.

The protected formulation is:

> **The retrospective defence may convert documentary continuity into evidential continuity.**

For example, repeated documentation can strongly establish that Sinemet was prescribed, that pain effects were recognised, that neuropathy was considered, or that PRKN was once discussed. It does not automatically establish that the relevant causal proposition was independently re-derived, that formulation/timing tested an OFF-state hypothesis, or that a provisional category was revalidated each time it reappeared.

This is best labelled **constructive defensibility** when facts are assembled into a coherent retrospective defence without evidence that the historical reasoning was as complete or linear as the retrospective account may suggest. It remains a documentary inference, not an allegation of deception.

### 129.15.2 Evidential tiers and stopping rule {#129-15-2-evidential-tiers-and-stopping-rule .anchored-heading}

The present evidential position should be held at the following levels:

1.  **Ordinary conscientious documentation --- strongly plausible.**
2.  **Prospective defensibility --- plausible and documentarily supported as a style/function.**
3.  **Defensive problem-framing / asymmetric epistemic documentation --- strongest serious hypothesis presently supported.**
4.  **Constructive retrospective defence --- meaningful and testable, but requires source-by-source checking.**
5.  **Institutional protective consolidation after recognition of error --- hypothesis only; not established.**
6.  **Deliberate concealment, falsification or deceptive record construction --- not supported by the present record.**

The anti-drift stopping rule is explicit:

> **Do not infer a moment of recognised error, institutional protection, concealment, or falsification unless internal contemporaneous material demonstrates the transition.**

To move materially from Tier 4 to Tier 5 would require a temporal sequence visible in internal evidence: open uncertainty before a particular point; recognition that the earlier interpretation may have materially affected care; and subsequent communications showing a decision to frame, restrict, consolidate or defend the earlier position rather than independently reopen it.

The relevant evidence is therefore institutional "backstage" material rather than another public-facing clinical letter: QPS communications, complaint-response drafts and tracked changes, SIMT/PAR materials, internal Neurology/Clinical Director correspondence, review-selection records, version histories, and evidence showing whether contrary sources were specifically considered.

The strongest rebuttal to Tier 5 would be internal material showing a transparent attempt to reconstruct the case from first principles, preserve inconvenient evidence, obtain independent movement-disorders expertise, and determine whether the earlier model was wrong.

### 129.15.3 Relationship to the core case object {#129-15-3-relationship-to-the-core-case-object .anchored-heading}

This branch does not replace the established mechanism. It sharpens one consequence of it:

> **operational closure can generate documentary continuity; documentary continuity can later supply retrospective defensibility; but neither step by itself proves that the unresolved relationships embedded in the original state were independently re-tested.**

The revised complete mechanism is therefore:

> **broad relational GP problem → narrower hospital task → factual recovery with altered relationship structure → asymmetric evidential resolution → operational closure ("As per above") → distributed residual ownership → classification/documentary inheritance → local assimilation/reclassification → documentary accretion → prospective rationale becomes retrospectively reusable → constructive retrospective defence ("As the letters show") → external reopening → later molecular resolution of one historically open branch.**

The strongest concise statement remains:

> **The record is strongest at showing why the chosen pathway made sense from inside the pathway; it is weaker at showing that the relationships left outside that pathway were equivalently re-derived as new evidence accumulated.**

------------------------------------------------------------------------
:::

::: {#section-core-thesis .section .reader-section .kind-current section="Core thesis and anti-drift rules"}
::: section-kicker
Core thesis and anti-drift rules
:::

### Core thesis --- tidied hierarchy {#core-thesis-tidied-hierarchy .anchored-heading}

The case is presently strongest when kept in four separate evidential layers. The first two are documentary; the third requires clinical expert adjudication; the fourth is a governance/institutional hypothesis requiring additional sources. None should be allowed to borrow certainty from the layer above it.

#### Layer 1 --- Established record architecture {#layer-1-established-record-architecture .anchored-heading}

The GP sent a broad, medication-naive relational problem involving feet, gait, tone, movement initiation, toe phenomena, dexterity, function and deterioration under an open "parkinsonian or other" question. Hospital Neurology then established the Parkinsonian syndrome at high evidential resolution. The same 2017 record also contained structural foot pathology, an observed pseudo-foot-drop gait described as somewhat suggestive of dystonic posture, painful foot phenomena, and a tentative separate/peripheral-neuropathic branch.

The record later shows that these branches did not receive equivalent evidential development. The Parkinsonian/genetic branch received detailed examination, imaging, DaT investigation, complex differential reasoning, genetic consideration and outside specialist input. The painful/structural/peripheral branch was plausible and partly supported, but the surviving record does not show an equally explicit proposition-by-proposition bridge discriminating dystonic/Parkinsonian, structural/mechanical and neuropathic components within the same lower-limb territory.

#### Layer 2 --- Strongest documentary inference {#layer-2-strongest-documentary-inference .anchored-heading}

The strongest case object is therefore:

> **A broad relational problem was contracted into a narrower specialist task; omitted material later re-entered as facts, but the relationships among those facts were reformatted and resolved at unequal evidential depth. That mixed state became operational before all explanatory relationships were closed. The resulting classifications, service destinations and management rationales became more portable than the provenance, uncertainty and reopening conditions that originally qualified them. Later cross-boundary evidence generated genuine local reassessment, but the surviving record more clearly documents local assimilation/reclassification than a first-principles reconstruction of the parent model.**

The shortest protected statement is:

> **The decomposition became durable before its clinical basis had been adequately established.**

The controlling internal contrast remains:

> **The problem is not lack of sophistication. It is where the sophistication was applied.**

And the same-author control remains:

> **When the model was fresh, uncertainty generated questions. Once the model was inherited, uncertainty was more often managed within it.**

"As per above" is treated as the operational hinge: not a declaration that uncertainty disappeared, but the point at which a mixed information state became sufficiently organised for treatment, referral and follow-up to proceed. "As the letters show" later illustrates how accumulated documentary continuity could acquire retrospective explanatory force.

#### Layer 3 --- Clinical expert questions generated by the documentary architecture {#layer-3-clinical-expert-questions-generated-by-the-documentary-architecture .anchored-heading}

The documentary case does not prove that the historical pain was dystonic, PRKN-mediated, neuropathic, mechanical, or caused by under-treatment. It does generate focused expert questions, especially whether the inherited partition later constrained how the following were assembled and tested:

-   the objectively documented dystonic-looking lower-limb sign;
-   specific contemporaneous PRKN/PARK2 suspicion;
-   the dramatic early procyclidine/rasagiline response and withdrawal observations;
-   later removal of Kemadrin/procyclidine because of ocular-pressure concern;
-   substitution with levodopa;
-   treatment-limiting chorea/dyskinesia and explicit attempts to keep levodopa exposure minimal;
-   later OFF-dystonia reasoning, evening under-coverage questions, morning foot symptoms and medication-state pain effects;
-   and the later reported marked improvement after sustained overnight dopaminergic coverage.

The protected therapeutic formulation is:

> **The question is not whether treatment response proves the cause of pain. It is whether repeated medication-state effects, dystonic lower-limb findings and treatment-limiting dyskinesia should have triggered structured testing of a dose-state lower-limb hypothesis.**

The DBS issue remains separate: the record preserves the 2021 statement that DBS was "only ever for the tremor element" and would "not help with anything else." The documentary fact is secure; whether that was an adequately accurate account of DBS for this patient is an expert information-quality question, not a conclusion that DBS should have been performed.

#### Layer 4 --- Governance and retrospective-defensibility questions {#layer-4-governance-and-retrospective-defensibility-questions .anchored-heading}

The record contains unusually detailed contemporaneous rationales for decisions that later became central to the March 2025 complaint response. This supports examination of **prospective defensibility** and **asymmetric epistemic documentation**: whether the record became especially effective at preserving the rationale for decisions actually made while remaining less developed in showing that excluded relationships were equivalently re-tested.

The strongest supported formulation is:

> **The record is unusually well equipped to defend the decisions that were made, but materially less well equipped to show that the relationships excluded from those decisions were equivalently tested.**

The related retrospective risk is:

> **The retrospective defence may convert documentary continuity into evidential continuity.**

This does not establish institutional bad faith. "Protective consolidation after recognised error" remains a hypothesis only, requiring internal contemporaneous evidence such as complaint-response drafts, QPS/SIMT/PAR material, tracked changes, internal emails, review-selection records or minutes showing when concern was recognised and how contrary evidence was handled. Deliberate concealment, falsification or deceptive record construction are not supported by the present record.

#### Present complete mechanism {#present-complete-mechanism .anchored-heading}

> **broad relational GP problem → narrower hospital task → factual recovery with altered relationship structure → asymmetric evidential resolution → operational closure ("As per above") → distributed residual ownership → classification/documentary inheritance → local assimilation/reclassification → documentary accretion → prospective rationale becomes retrospectively reusable → constructive retrospective defence ("As the letters show") → external reopening → later molecular resolution of one historically open branch**

#### Motive firewall {#motive-firewall .anchored-heading}

The case should not presently be pleaded or framed as a motive case. Do not infer recognised error, conscious protection, concealment, falsification, dishonesty, deliberate diagnostic persistence or institutional self-protection from the documentary architecture alone. The current evidence can support a question about **protective effects of the information architecture** without supporting a conclusion about protective intent.

### Anti-drift rule {#anti-drift-rule .anchored-heading}

From this version onward, new material may **strengthen, qualify, weaken, or falsify** the current formulation, but it should not silently change the object of the case. The object remains the **information pathway**: how the presenting problem was framed, which relationships were investigated at high or low resolution, how unresolved material became operational, what became portable through later documentation, what reopened locally, and whether local revisions ever became global reconstruction.

The file should therefore resist drift into simpler but weaker narratives such as:

-   "the feet were ignored";
-   "the neurologist was unsophisticated";
-   "peripheral neuropathy was simply wrong";
-   "PRKN should obviously have been diagnosed in 2017";
-   "the 2026 result proves what clinicians should have known earlier";
-   or "every later treatment response proves neurological causation."

The controlling formulations remain:

> **The problem is not lack of sophistication. It is where the sophistication was applied.**

> **The counterfactual is not different diagnosis. It is different investigative object.**

> **The facts came back. The relationships did not come back with equal force.**

> **"As per above" did not make the uncertainties disappear; it made an arrangement of those uncertainties operationally durable.**

> **The inherited architecture was permeable, not frozen: later evidence generated genuine local reassessment, but the surviving record more clearly documents local updating than global reconstruction.**

> **The critical transformation occurred not when the facts disappeared, but between their recovery and their synthesis.**

> **The record is less a story of facts being forgotten than of relationships being reformatted once, then inherited.**

> **"As per above" marks operational closure before epistemic closure was complete; "As the letters show" later demonstrates how documentary continuity could acquire retrospective explanatory authority.**

> **The GP kept seeing the arrows; specialist care owned the authority to decide what they meant.**

And the controlling counterfactual is deliberately narrower than a prediction of diagnostic outcome:

> **The counterfactual is not necessarily a different diagnosis. It is a different investigative object --- a different question architecture.**

The purpose of this file is to stage that formulation while preserving the distinction between record fact, documentary inference, clinical expert question, and legal conclusion.

Main evidential analysis
